MixtureDefinitions.mixture_label =
fun (n N : nat) (s : tuple.tuple_of n (fintype.ordinal N)) (i : nat) =>
@seq.nth nat O
  (@seq.map
     (@eqtype.SubType.sort nat (fun x : nat => ssrnat.leq (S x) N)
        (fintype.fintype_ordinal__canonical__eqtype_SubType N))
     nat
     (@eqtype.isSub.val_subdef nat (fun x : nat => ssrnat.leq (S x) N)
        (@eqtype.SubType.sort nat (fun x : nat => ssrnat.leq (S x) N)
           (fintype.fintype_ordinal__canonical__eqtype_SubType N))
        (@eqtype.SubType.eqtype_isSub_mixin _ _ _
           (@eqtype.SubType.phant_on_ nat (fun x : nat => ssrnat.leq (S x) N)
              (fintype.fintype_ordinal__canonical__eqtype_SubType N)
              (ssreflect.Phant
                 (@eqtype.SubType.sort nat
                    (fun x : nat => ssrnat.leq (S x) N)
                    (fintype.fintype_ordinal__canonical__eqtype_SubType N)))
            :
            @eqtype.SubType.axioms_ nat (fun x : nat => ssrnat.leq (S x) N)
              (@eqtype.SubType.sort nat (fun x : nat => ssrnat.leq (S x) N)
                 (fintype.fintype_ordinal__canonical__eqtype_SubType N)))))
     (@tuple.tval n (fintype.ordinal N) s))
  i
     : forall {n N : nat} (_ : tuple.tuple_of n (fintype.ordinal N))
         (_ : nat),
       nat
